    15	/**
    16	 * Self-service bank coordinates (RIB / titulaire / CIN) for a collaborator. Each field locks
    17	 * once saved (only an admin can change a locked field). Lives on the self-profile page.
    18	 */
    19	export const BankDetailsCard: React.FC<{ profile: BankProfile; currentUserId: string | null; chapter?: string }> = ({ profile, currentUserId, chapter = '02' }) => {
    20	    const { toast } = useToast();
    21	    const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    22	
    23	    const [draft, setDraft] = useState({
    24	        bank_name: '',
    25	        bank_account_holder: '',
    26	        bank_rib: '',
    27	        cin_number: '',
    28	    });
    29	
    30	    useEffect(() => {
    31	        setDraft({
    32	            bank_name: profile?.bank_name || '',
    33	            bank_account_holder: profile?.bank_account_holder || '',
    34	            bank_rib: profile?.bank_rib || '',
    35	            cin_number: profile?.cin_number || '',
    36	        });
    37	    }, [profile?.bank_name, profile?.bank_account_holder, profile?.bank_rib, profile?.cin_number]);
    38	
    39	    const lock = {
    40	        bank_name: !!profile?.bank_name,
    41	        bank_account_holder: !!profile?.bank_account_holder,
    42	        bank_rib: !!profile?.bank_rib,
    43	        cin_number: !!profile?.cin_number,
    44	    };
    45	
    46	    const anyEditable = !lock.bank_name || !lock.bank_account_holder || !lock.bank_rib || !lock.cin_number;
    47	    const allLocked = lock.bank_name && lock.bank_account_holder && lock.bank_rib && lock.cin_number;
    48	
    49	    const hasDirtyChanges = useMemo(() => {
    50	        if (!lock.bank_name && draft.bank_name.trim() !== (profile?.bank_name || '')) return true;
    51	        if (!lock.bank_account_holder && draft.bank_account_holder.trim() !== (profile?.bank_account_holder || '')) return true;
    52	        if (!lock.bank_rib && draft.bank_rib.trim() !== (profile?.bank_rib || '')) return true;
    53	        if (!lock.cin_number && draft.cin_number.trim() !== (profile?.cin_number || '')) return true;
    54	        return false;
    55	    }, [draft, profile, lock]);
    56	
    57	    const saveMutation = useMutation({
    58	        mutationFn: async () => {
    59	            if (!currentUserId) throw new Error('Non connecté');
    60	            const patch: Record<string, string> = {};
    61	            if (!lock.bank_name && draft.bank_name.trim()) patch.bank_name = draft.bank_name.trim();
    62	            if (!lock.bank_account_holder && draft.bank_account_holder.trim()) patch.bank_account_holder = draft.bank_account_holder.trim();
    63	            if (!lock.bank_rib && draft.bank_rib.trim()) patch.bank_rib = draft.bank_rib.trim();
    64	            if (!lock.cin_number && draft.cin_number.trim()) patch.cin_number = draft.cin_number.trim();
    65	
    66	            if (Object.keys(patch).length === 0) throw new Error('Rien à enregistrer');
    67	
    68	            const { error } = await (supabase as any).from('users').update(patch).eq('id', currentUserId);
    69	            if (error) throw error;
    70	        },
    71	        onSuccess: () => {
    72	            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['profile-self'] });
    73	            toast({ title: 'Coordonnées enregistrées', description: 'Les champs sauvegardés sont verrouillés. Contactez un admin pour les modifier.' });
    74	        },
    75	        onError: (err: any) => toast({ title: 'Échec', description: err.message, variant: 'destructive' }),
    76	    });
    77	
    78	    return (
    79	        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-ant-line bg-ant-paper overflow-hidden">
    80	            <div className="h-[2px] w-full bg-ant-orange/70" />
    81	            <div className="px-5 md:px-6 py-4 md:py-5 border-b border-ant-line flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
    82	                <div>
    83	                    <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.24em] text-ant-mid">
    84	                        <span className="font-display text-ant-orange font-semibold">{chapter}</span> Coordonnées bancaires
    85	                    </div>
    86	                    <p className="font-serif italic text-[12px] text-ant-mid mt-1">
    87	                        Indispensables pour recevoir vos paiements
    88	                    </p>
    89	                </div>
    90	                {allLocked && (
    91	                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 h-7 px-2.5 rounded-full border border-ant-line bg-ant-light text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ant-mid shrink-0">
    92	                        <Lock className="w-3 h-3" />
    93	                        Verrouillé
    94	                    </span>
    95	                )}
    96	            </div>
    97	
    98	            <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-4">
    99	                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
   100	                    <EditableBankField
   101	                        label="Banque"
   102	                        icon={CreditCard}
   103	                        value={draft.bank_name}
   104	                        onChange={(v) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, bank_name: v }))}
   105	                        locked={lock.bank_name}
   106	                        placeholder="ex. Attijariwafa Bank"
   107	                    />
   108	                    <EditableBankField
   109	                        label="Titulaire du compte"
   110	                        value={draft.bank_account_holder}
   111	                        onChange={(v) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, bank_account_holder: v }))}
   112	                        locked={lock.bank_account_holder}
   113	                        placeholder="Nom légal complet"
   114	                    />
   115	                    <EditableBankField
   116	                        label="RIB"
   117	                        value={draft.bank_rib}
   118	                        onChange={(v) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, bank_rib: v }))}
   119	                        locked={lock.bank_rib}
   120	                        placeholder="24 chiffres"
   121	                        mono
   122	                    />
   123	                    <EditableBankField
   124	                        label="CIN"
   125	                        value={draft.cin_number}
   126	                        onChange={(v) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, cin_number: v }))}
   127	                        locked={lock.cin_number}
   128	                        placeholder="Numéro carte d'identité"
   129	                    />
   130	                </div>
   131	
   132	                {anyEditable && (
   133	                    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-3 pt-2 border-t border-ant-line">
   134	                        <p className="font-serif italic text-[11px] text-ant-mid max-w-md">
   135	                            Une fois enregistrés, ces champs sont verrouillés. Pour les modifier plus tard, contactez un admin.
   136	                        </p>
   137	                        <button
   138	                            type="button"
   139	                            onClick={() => saveMutation.mutate()}
   140	                            disabled={!hasDirtyChanges || saveMutation.isPending}
   141	                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 h-10 px-4 rounded-full bg-ant-ink text-ant-light text-[12px] font-medium hover:bg-ant-ink/95 disabled:opacity-60 transition-all shadow-[0_10px_24px_-10px_rgba(20,20,19,0.5)] shrink-0"
   142	                        >
   143	                            {saveMutation.isPending ? <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" /> : <Save className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
   144	                            Enregistrer
   145	                        </button>
   146	                    </div>
   147	                )}
   148	
   149	                {!allLocked && (
   150	                    <div className="rounded-xl border border-ant-orange/25 bg-ant-orange/5 p-3 flex items-start gap-2">
   151	                        <Info className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-ant-orange" />
   152	                        <p className="font-serif italic text-[11px] text-ant-ink leading-relaxed">
   153	                            Vos coordonnées bancaires sont incomplètes. L'administrateur en a besoin pour vous verser vos gains.
   154	                        </p>
   155	                    </div>
   156	                )}
   157	            </div>
   158	        </section>
   159	    );
   160	};
   161	
   162	const EditableBankField: React.FC<{
   163	    label: string;
   164	    icon?: typeof CreditCard;
   165	    value: string;
   166	    onChange: (v: string) => void;
   167	    locked: boolean;
   168	    placeholder?: string;
   169	    mono?: boolean;
   170	}> = ({ label, value, onChange, locked, placeholder, mono }) => (
   171	    <div className={cn(
   172	        'rounded-xl p-3 border space-y-1.5 transition-colors',
   173	        locked
   174	            ? 'bg-ant-light border-ant-line'
   175	            : 'bg-ant-paper border-ant-line focus-within:border-ant-ink focus-within:ring-2 focus-within:ring-ant-orange/20',
   176	    )}>
   177	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   178	            <p className="text-[9px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-ant-mid">{label}</p>
   179	            {locked && <Lock className="w-3 h-3 text-ant-mid/70" />}
   180	        </div>
     1	// @ts-nocheck
     2	// Public: verifies the OTP, then applies the new email and/or password via GoTrue admin.
     3	// New credentials transit only here (never stored). Single-use. Sends confirmations:
     4	// password_changed → old email; email_changed → BOTH new and old (anti-takeover notice).
     5	import "jsr:@supabase/functions-js/edge-runtime.d.ts";
     6	import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2.39.3";
     7	
     8	const corsHeaders = {
     9	  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
    10	  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
    11	};
    12	const json = (o: unknown, s: number) =>
    13	  new Response(JSON.stringify(o), { headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" }, status: s });
    14	const TOKEN_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,80}$/;
    15	const sha256Hex = async (s: string) =>
    16	  Array.from(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(s))))
    17	    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
    18	const clientIp = (req: Request) => (req.headers.get("x-forwarded-for") || "").split(",")[0].trim();
    19	const nowFR = () => { try { return new Date().toLocaleString("fr-FR", { timeZone: "Africa/Casablanca", dateStyle: "long", timeStyle: "short" }); } catch { return new Date().toISOString(); } };
    20	
    21	async function sendEmail(payload: Record<string, unknown>) {
    22	  const SERVICE = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "";
    23	  return fetch(`${Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")}/functions/v1/send-email`, {
    24	    method: "POST",
    25	    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": `Bearer ${SERVICE}`, "apikey": SERVICE },
    26	    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    27	  }).catch((e) => console.error("send-email call failed:", e));
    28	}
    29	
    30	Deno.serve(async (req) => {
    31	  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
    32	  try {
    33	    const url = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "";
    34	    const service = createClient(url, Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "", {
    35	      auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false },
    36	    });
    37	
    38	    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    39	    const token = String(body?.token ?? "");
    40	    const otp = String(body?.otp ?? "").trim();
    41	    const newEmail = body?.new_email ? String(body.new_email).trim().toLowerCase() : null;
    42	    const newPassword = body?.new_password ? String(body.new_password) : null;
    43	    const origin = typeof body?.origin === "string" && /^https?:\/\//.test(body.origin)
    44	      ? body.origin.replace(/\/$/, "") : (Deno.env.get("SITE_URL") ?? "");
    45	
    46	    if (!TOKEN_RE.test(token)) return json({ ok: false, reason: "invalid" }, 400);
    47	    if (!newEmail && !newPassword) return json({ ok: false, reason: "nothing_to_change" }, 400);
    48	    if (newEmail && !/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(newEmail)) return json({ ok: false, reason: "invalid_email" }, 400);
    49	    if (newPassword && newPassword.length < 6) return json({ ok: false, reason: "weak_password" }, 400);
    50	
    51	    const tokenHash = await sha256Hex(token);
    52	    const { data: row } = await service.from("credential_change_requests")
    53	      .select("*").eq("token_hash", `\\x${tokenHash}`).is("consumed_at", null).gt("expires_at", new Date().toISOString()).maybeSingle();
    54	    if (!row) return json({ ok: false, reason: "invalid_or_used" }, 400);
    55	
    56	    // OTP checks
    57	    if (!row.otp_hash || !row.otp_expires_at || new Date(row.otp_expires_at) < new Date())
    58	      return json({ ok: false, reason: "otp_expired" }, 400);
    59	    if ((row.otp_attempts ?? 0) >= 5) return json({ ok: false, reason: "too_many_attempts" }, 429);
    60	    const otpHashHex = await sha256Hex(otp);
    61	    const storedHex = String(row.otp_hash).replace(/^\\x/, "");
    62	    if (otpHashHex !== storedHex) {
    63	      await service.from("credential_change_requests").update({ otp_attempts: (row.otp_attempts ?? 0) + 1 }).eq("id", row.id);
    64	      return json({ ok: false, reason: "otp_invalid" }, 400);
    65	    }
    66	
    67	    const { data: u } = await service.from("users").select("email, name").eq("id", row.user_id).single();
    68	    const oldEmail = u?.email ?? null;
    69	    const name = u?.name || "";
    70	
    71	    // Apply via GoTrue admin
    72	    const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    73	    if (newEmail) { updates.email = newEmail; updates.email_confirm = true; }
    74	    if (newPassword) { updates.password = newPassword; }
    75	    const { error: authErr } = await service.auth.admin.updateUserById(row.user_id, updates);
    76	    if (authErr) {
    77	      const dup = /registered|exist|duplicate/i.test(JSON.stringify(authErr));
    78	      return json({ ok: false, reason: dup ? "email_taken" : "apply_failed", detail: authErr.message }, dup ? 409 : 400);
    79	    }
    80	    if (newEmail) await service.from("users").update({ email: newEmail }).eq("id", row.user_id);
    81	
    82	    // Single-use: mark consumed (atomic guard).
    83	    await service.from("credential_change_requests").update({ consumed_at: new Date().toISOString() })
    84	      .eq("id", row.id).is("consumed_at", null);
    85	
    86	    // Confirmations
    87	    const ip = clientIp(req);
    88	    const changed_at = nowFR();
    89	    if (newPassword && oldEmail) {
    90	      await sendEmail({ key: "password_changed", to: oldEmail, user_id: row.user_id,
    91	        vars: { user_name: name, changed_at, secure_url: origin ? `${origin}/auth` : "/auth", request_ip: ip } });
    92	    }
    93	    if (newEmail) {
    94	      const ev = { user_name: name, old_email: oldEmail ?? "", new_email: newEmail, changed_at, request_ip: ip };
    95	      await sendEmail({ key: "email_changed", to: newEmail, user_id: row.user_id, vars: ev });
    96	      if (oldEmail && oldEmail.toLowerCase() !== newEmail) {
    97	        await sendEmail({ key: "email_changed", to: oldEmail, user_id: row.user_id, vars: ev });
    98	      }
    99	    }
   100	
   101	    return json({ ok: true, email_changed: !!newEmail, password_changed: !!newPassword }, 200);
   102	  } catch (e) {
   103	    return json({ ok: false, reason: "error", detail: (e as Error).message }, 500);
   104	  }
   105	});
     1	// @ts-nocheck
     2	// Authenticated: verifies the latest OTP issued to the logged-in user, then:
     3	//  - with new_email/new_password → applies the change (GoTrue admin) + confirmation emails;
     4	//  - with neither → just verifies (email-2FA login gate).
     5	import "jsr:@supabase/functions-js/edge-runtime.d.ts";
     6	import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2.39.3";
     7	
     8	const corsHeaders = {
     9	  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
    10	  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
    11	};
    12	const json = (o: unknown, s: number) =>
    13	  new Response(JSON.stringify(o), { headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" }, status: s });
    14	const sha256Hex = async (s: string) =>
    15	  Array.from(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(s))))
    16	    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
    17	const clientIp = (req: Request) => (req.headers.get("x-forwarded-for") || "").split(",")[0].trim();
    18	const nowFR = () => { try { return new Date().toLocaleString("fr-FR", { timeZone: "Africa/Casablanca", dateStyle: "long", timeStyle: "short" }); } catch { return new Date().toISOString(); } };
    19	
    20	async function sendEmail(payload: Record<string, unknown>) {
    21	  const SERVICE = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "";
    22	  await fetch(`${Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")}/functions/v1/send-email`, {
    23	    method: "POST",
    24	    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": `Bearer ${SERVICE}`, "apikey": SERVICE },
    25	    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    26	  }).catch((e) => console.error("send-email call failed:", e));
    27	}

    29	Deno.serve(async (req) => {
    30	  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
    31	  try {
    32	    const url = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "";
    33	    const service = createClient(url, Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "", {
    34	      auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false },
    35	    });
    36	    const jwt = (req.headers.get("Authorization") ?? "").replace(/^bearer\s+/i, "").trim();
    37	    const { data: { user }, error: uErr } = await service.auth.getUser(jwt);
    38	    if (uErr || !user) return json({ ok: false, reason: "unauthenticated" }, 401);
    39	
    40	    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    41	    const otp = String(body?.otp ?? "").trim();
    42	    const newEmail = body?.new_email ? String(body.new_email).trim().toLowerCase() : null;
    43	    const newPassword = body?.new_password ? String(body.new_password) : null;
    44	    const origin = typeof body?.origin === "string" && /^https?:\/\//.test(body.origin)
    45	      ? body.origin.replace(/\/$/, "") : (Deno.env.get("SITE_URL") ?? "");
    46	
    47	    if (newEmail && !/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(newEmail)) return json({ ok: false, reason: "invalid_email" }, 400);
    48	    if (newPassword && newPassword.length < 6) return json({ ok: false, reason: "weak_password" }, 400);
    49	
    50	    // Latest OTP issued to this user.
    51	    const { data: row } = await service.from("credential_change_requests")
    52	      .select("*").eq("user_id", user.id).is("consumed_at", null).not("otp_hash", "is", null)
    53	      .order("created_at", { ascending: false }).limit(1).maybeSingle();
    54	    if (!row || !row.otp_expires_at || new Date(row.otp_expires_at) < new Date())
    55	      return json({ ok: false, reason: "otp_expired" }, 400);
    56	    if ((row.otp_attempts ?? 0) >= 5) return json({ ok: false, reason: "too_many_attempts" }, 429);
    57	    if (await sha256Hex(otp) !== String(row.otp_hash).replace(/^\\x/, "")) {
    58	      await service.from("credential_change_requests").update({ otp_attempts: (row.otp_attempts ?? 0) + 1 }).eq("id", row.id);
    59	      return json({ ok: false, reason: "otp_invalid" }, 400);
    60	    }
    61	
    62	    const { data: me } = await service.from("users").select("email, name").eq("id", user.id).single();
    63	    const oldEmail = me?.email ?? user.email ?? null;
    64	    const name = me?.name || "";
    65	
    66	    // Apply credential change if requested.
    67	    if (newEmail || newPassword) {
    68	      const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    69	      if (newEmail) { updates.email = newEmail; updates.email_confirm = true; }
    70	      if (newPassword) { updates.password = newPassword; }
    71	      const { error: authErr } = await service.auth.admin.updateUserById(user.id, updates);
    72	      if (authErr) {
    73	        const dup = /registered|exist|duplicate/i.test(JSON.stringify(authErr));
    74	        return json({ ok: false, reason: dup ? "email_taken" : "apply_failed", detail: authErr.message }, dup ? 409 : 400);
    75	      }
    76	      if (newEmail) await service.from("users").update({ email: newEmail }).eq("id", user.id);
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    await service.from("credential_change_requests").update({ consumed_at: new Date().toISOString() })
    80	      .eq("id", row.id).is("consumed_at", null);
    81	
    82	    const ip = clientIp(req); const changed_at = nowFR();
    83	    if (newPassword && oldEmail) {
    84	      await sendEmail({ key: "password_changed", to: oldEmail, user_id: user.id,
    85	        vars: { user_name: name, changed_at, secure_url: origin ? `${origin}/auth` : "/auth", request_ip: ip } });
    86	    }

    92	      }
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    return json({ ok: true, verified: true, email_changed: !!newEmail, password_changed: !!newPassword }, 200);
    96	  } catch (e) {
    97	    return json({ ok: false, reason: "error", detail: (e as Error).message }, 500);
    98	  }

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	-- Security remediation 2026-07-03 (self-owned self-hosted production box).
     2	-- Enforces column/row/tenant authorization controls surfaced by the internal
     3	-- secure-coding review. All changes are non-destructive to data.
     4	--
     5	-- Design note on the guard triggers (R2/R5/R6): they are SECURITY INVOKER so
     6	-- that current_user reflects the ACTUAL executing role. This lets them exempt
     7	-- non-end-user writers cleanly:
     8	--   * service_role  -> manage-users / provider webhooks / admin edge flows
     9	--   * postgres      -> SECURITY DEFINER fns (sync_imported_orders, approve_sheet_order)
    10	--   * cron          -> scheduled jobs
    11	-- Only a real `authenticated` self-write is gated. (A SECURITY DEFINER trigger
    12	-- would see current_user='postgres' and auth.uid()=NULL for service_role, which
    13	-- would WRONGLY block legitimate admin edits made through manage-users.)
    14	
    15	BEGIN;
    16	
    17	-- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    18	-- Shared helper: is the acting user a business admin?
    19	-- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    20	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.current_user_is_business_admin()
    21	RETURNS boolean
    22	LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public
    23	AS $$
    24	  SELECT EXISTS (
    25	    SELECT 1 FROM public.users u
    26	    WHERE u.id = auth.uid() AND u.role IN ('super admin','admin')
    27	  );
    28	$$;
    29	REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.current_user_is_business_admin() FROM public;
    30	GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.current_user_is_business_admin() TO authenticated;
    31	
    32	-- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    33	-- R2 — users: column-level UPDATE grant + privileged-column guard
    34	-- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    35	REVOKE UPDATE ON public.users FROM authenticated;
    36	GRANT UPDATE (
    37	  name, username, phone, address,
    38	  avatar_url, avatar_config,
    39	  bank_name, bank_account_holder, bank_rib, cin_number,
    40	  courier_parcel_fragile, courier_parcel_open, courier_parcel_try_on,
    41	  updated_at
    42	) ON public.users TO authenticated;
    43	
    44	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_users_self_privileged_cols()
    45	RETURNS trigger
    46	LANGUAGE plpgsql SET search_path = public, pg_temp
    47	AS $$
    48	BEGIN
    49	  -- Exempt non-end-user writers (see header). Only gate real self-edits.
    50	  IF current_user <> 'authenticated' THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;
    51	  IF public.current_user_is_business_admin() THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;
    52	
    53	  IF ( NEW.role              IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.role
    54	    OR NEW."DigiCom Business" IS DISTINCT FROM OLD."DigiCom Business"
    55	    OR NEW.permissions       IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.permissions
    56	    OR NEW.commission_price  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.commission_price
    57	    OR NEW.commission_basis  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.commission_basis
    58	    OR NEW.salary_amount     IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.salary_amount
    59	    OR NEW.payment_type      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.payment_type
    60	    OR NEW.active            IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.active ) THEN
    61	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'privileged column change not permitted on self-update'
    62	      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
    63	  END IF;
    64	  RETURN NEW;
    65	END $$;
    66	
    67	DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_guard_users_self_privileged_cols ON public.users;
    68	CREATE TRIGGER trg_guard_users_self_privileged_cols
    69	  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.users FOR EACH ROW
    70	  EXECUTE FUNCTION public.guard_users_self_privileged_cols();
    71	
    72	-- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    73	-- R4 — courier payout notes: split tenant-read from admin-only write
    74	-- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    75	DROP POLICY IF EXISTS courier_payment_notes_tenant_all ON public.courier_payment_notes;
    76	CREATE POLICY courier_payment_notes_tenant_select ON public.courier_payment_notes
    77	  FOR SELECT TO authenticated
    78	  USING ("DigiCom Business" = (SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id()));
    79	CREATE POLICY courier_payment_notes_admin_write ON public.courier_payment_notes
    80	  FOR ALL TO authenticated
    81	  USING     ("DigiCom Business" = (SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id())
    82	             AND (SELECT check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin','admin'])))
    83	  WITH CHECK ("DigiCom Business" = (SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id())
    84	             AND (SELECT check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin','admin'])));
    85	
    86	DROP POLICY IF EXISTS courier_payment_note_orders_tenant_all ON public.courier_payment_note_orders;
    87	CREATE POLICY courier_payment_note_orders_tenant_select ON public.courier_payment_note_orders
    88	  FOR SELECT TO authenticated
    89	  USING ("DigiCom Business" = (SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id()));
    90	CREATE POLICY courier_payment_note_orders_admin_write ON public.courier_payment_note_orders
    91	  FOR ALL TO authenticated
    92	  USING     ("DigiCom Business" = (SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id())
    93	             AND (SELECT check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin','admin'])))
    94	  WITH CHECK ("DigiCom Business" = (SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id())
    95	             AND (SELECT check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin','admin'])));
    96	
    97	-- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    98	-- R5 — orders: ownership-scoped policies + money/attribution column freeze
    99	--   Sellers (role 'commercial') legitimately CREATE/EDIT orders and set
   100	--   price/raw_price (order total). price/raw_price do NOT feed seller
   101	--   commission (which = users.commission_price x units gated by is_delivered
   102	--   + seller_id), so they are NOT frozen. The attribution+eligibility columns
   103	--   that DO drive the commission ledger — seller_id and a delivered
   104	--   package_status_id — are frozen for non-admins.
   105	-- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   106	DROP POLICY IF EXISTS orders_tenant_insert ON public.orders;
   107	CREATE POLICY orders_tenant_insert ON public.orders
   108	  FOR INSERT TO authenticated
   109	  WITH CHECK (
   110	    "DigiCom Business" = (SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id())
   285	ALTER POLICY "seller_payments_insert_admin" ON public.seller_payments
   286	  WITH CHECK ((("DigiCom Business" = (select current_user_digicom_business_id())) AND (select check_user_role(( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid), ARRAY['super admin'::text, 'admin'::text]))));
   287	ALTER POLICY "seller_payments_select_scoped" ON public.seller_payments
   288	  USING (((seller_id = ( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid)) OR (("DigiCom Business" = (select current_user_digicom_business_id())) AND (select check_user_role(( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid), ARRAY['super admin'::text, 'admin'::text])))));
   289	ALTER POLICY "seller_payments_update_admin" ON public.seller_payments
   290	  USING ((("DigiCom Business" = (select current_user_digicom_business_id())) AND (select check_user_role(( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid), ARRAY['super admin'::text, 'admin'::text]))))
   291	  WITH CHECK ((("DigiCom Business" = (select current_user_digicom_business_id())) AND (select check_user_role(( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid), ARRAY['super admin'::text, 'admin'::text]))));

  2829	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  2832	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS users_one_business_owner_per_biz
  2833	  ON public.users ("DigiCom Business")

  2837	
  2838	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  2839	-- 5. Protection trigger: the business owner cannot be demoted (role changed
  2840	--    away from 'business owner') or deleted by a peer. SECURITY DEFINER so
  2841	--    it can read the acting user's own role row regardless of RLS; search_path
  2842	--    pinned; EXECUTE revoked from PUBLIC/anon/authenticated (callers reach it
  2843	--    only via the trigger, never directly) — box default ACL grants EXECUTE
  2844	--    to everyone by default, so this revoke is not optional.
  2845	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  2846	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.protect_business_owner_role()
  2847	RETURNS trigger
  2848	LANGUAGE plpgsql
  2849	SECURITY DEFINER
  2850	SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_catalog'
  2851	AS $$
  2852	DECLARE
  2853	    v_actor_role text;
  2854	    v_demote_or_delete boolean;
  2855	    v_become_owner boolean;
  2856	BEGIN
  2857	    IF TG_RELID <> 'public.users'::regclass THEN
  2858	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'protect_business_owner_role: unexpected relation %', TG_RELID;
  2859	    END IF;
  2860	
  2861	    v_demote_or_delete :=
  2862	        (TG_OP = 'DELETE' AND OLD.role = 'business owner')
  2863	        OR (TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND OLD.role = 'business owner' AND NEW.role IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.role);
  2864	    v_become_owner :=
  2865	        TG_OP IN ('INSERT', 'UPDATE') AND NEW.role = 'business owner'
  2866	        AND (TG_OP = 'INSERT' OR OLD.role IS DISTINCT FROM 'business owner');
  2867	
  2868	    -- Fast path: the vast majority of users writes touch neither guard.
  2869	    IF NOT (v_demote_or_delete OR v_become_owner) THEN
  2870	        RETURN CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD ELSE NEW END;
  2871	    END IF;
  2872	
  2873	    -- System/service-role sessions pass (edge fns enforce their own caller
  2874	    -- checks; migrations/backfills run as postgres).
  2875	    IF auth.uid() IS NULL THEN
  2876	        RETURN CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD ELSE NEW END;
  2877	    END IF;
  2878	
  2879	    SELECT role::text INTO v_actor_role FROM public.users WHERE id = auth.uid();
  2880	
  2881	    IF v_actor_role IS DISTINCT FROM 'owner' THEN
  2882	        IF v_demote_or_delete THEN
  2883	            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Le propriétaire de l''entreprise ne peut pas être modifié par un pair.';
  2884	        ELSE
  2885	            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Seule la plateforme peut désigner un propriétaire d''entreprise.';
  2886	        END IF;
  2887	    END IF;
  2888	
  2889	    RETURN CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD ELSE NEW END;
  2890	END;
  2891	$$;
  2892	
  2893	REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.protect_business_owner_role() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
  2894	
  2895	DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_protect_business_owner_role ON public.users;
  2896	DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_protect_business_owner ON public.users;
  2897	CREATE TRIGGER trg_protect_business_owner
  2898	    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON public.users
  2899	    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.protect_business_owner_role();
  2900	
  2901	-- ── whatsapp.profiles view (live-sweep miss, found during authoring) ────────
  2902	-- Two role arrays gate WhatsApp profile visibility/bucketing; without parity a
  2903	-- business owner disappears from the WhatsApp module entirely. Live def dumped
  2904	-- 2026-07-29; ONLY the two arrays changed. security_barrier preserved in WITH.
  2905	CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW whatsapp.profiles WITH (security_barrier = true) AS
  2906	 SELECT id,
  2907	    email,
  2908	    name AS full_name,
  2909	        CASE
  2910	            WHEN role = ANY (ARRAY['super admin'::user_role, 'business owner'::user_role, 'admin'::user_role]) THEN 'admin'::text
  2911	            ELSE 'user'::text
  2912	        END AS role,
  2913	    COALESCE(active, true) AS is_active,
  2914	    avatar_config,
  2915	    created_at,
  2916	    avatar_url
  2917	   FROM users u
  2918	  WHERE "DigiCom Business" = current_user_digicom_business_id() AND (role = ANY (ARRAY['super admin'::user_role, 'business owner'::user_role, 'admin'::user_role, 'commercial'::user_role])) AND (current_user_is_business_admin() OR id = auth.uid());
  2919	
  2920	COMMIT;
     1	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
     2	
     3	function dataURLtoBlob(dataUrl: string): Blob {
     4	    const [head, b64] = dataUrl.split(',');
     5	    const mime = /:(.*?);/.exec(head)?.[1] || 'image/png';
     6	    const bin = atob(b64);
     7	    const arr = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
     8	    for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) arr[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
     9	    return new Blob([arr], { type: mime });
    10	}
    11	
    12	/**
    13	 * Save the logged-in user's own avatar. Uploaded photo → `avatar` bucket (avatar_url);
    14	 * generated avatar → avatar_config. The other is cleared so UserAvatar shows the new one.
    15	 */
    16	export async function saveOwnAvatar(v: { config?: object | null; dataUrl?: string | null }): Promise<void> {
    17	    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    18	    if (!user) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
    19	
    20	    if (v.dataUrl) {
    21	        const path = `self/${user.id}.png`;
    22	        const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage.from('avatar')
    23	            .upload(path, dataURLtoBlob(v.dataUrl), { upsert: true, contentType: 'image/png' });
    24	        if (upErr) throw upErr;
    25	        const base = supabase.storage.from('avatar').getPublicUrl(path).data.publicUrl;
    26	        const url = `${base}?v=${Date.now()}`; // cache-bust the stable path
    27	        const { error } = await supabase.from('users')
    28	            .update({ avatar_url: url, avatar_config: null } as never).eq('id', user.id);
    29	        if (error) throw error;
    30	    } else if (v.config) {
    31	        const { error } = await supabase.from('users')
    32	            .update({ avatar_config: v.config, avatar_url: null } as never).eq('id', user.id);
    33	        if (error) throw error;
    34	    }
    35	}
    36	
    37	/** Save the logged-in user's own basic identity fields. */
    38	export async function saveOwnBasicInfo(patch: { name?: string; username?: string; phone?: string }): Promise<void> {
    39	    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    40	    if (!user) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
    41	    const { error } = await supabase.from('users').update(patch as never).eq('id', user.id);
    42	    if (error) throw error;
    43	}

    37	                    onClick={logout}

    47	
    48	export const ProtectedRoute = ({ children, allowedRoles }: ProtectedRouteProps) => {
    49	    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    50	    const [authenticated, setAuthenticated] = useState(false);
    51	    // Page permissions are resolved from the SAME profile fetch as the role — no extra query.
    52	    const [role, setRole] = useState<string | null>(null);
    53	    const [pageMap, setPageMap] = useState<PagePermissionMap>(undefined);
    54	    const navigate = useNavigate();
    55	    const location = useLocation();
    56	
    57	    useEffect(() => {
    58	        const checkAuth = async () => {
    59	            const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
    60	
    61	            if (!session) {
    62	                navigate('/auth');
    63	                setLoading(false);
    64	                return;
    65	            }
    66	
    67	            // Always fetch the user profile to check active status
    68	            const { data: userProfile, error } = await supabase
    69	                .from('users')
    70	                .select('role, active, permissions')
    71	                .eq('id', session.user.id)
    72	                .single();
    73	
    74	            if (error || !userProfile) {
    75	                console.error("Error fetching user profile or profile not found:", error);
    76	                navigate('/auth');
    77	                setLoading(false);
    78	                return;
    79	            }
    80	
    81	            // Block disabled accounts: sign them out immediately
    82	            if (userProfile.active === false) {
    83	                await supabase.auth.signOut();
    84	                localStorage.removeItem('activeBusinessId');
    85	                navigate('/auth');
    86	                setLoading(false);
    87	                return;
    88	            }
    89	
    90	            if (allowedRoles && allowedRoles.length > 0) {
    91	                if (!allowedRoles.includes(userProfile.role)) {
    92	                    if (userProfile.role === 'commercial') {
    93	                        navigate('/seller/dashboard');
    94	                    } else if (userProfile.role === 'owner') {
    95	                        navigate('/owner/dashboard');
    96	                    } else if (userProfile.role === 'courier') {
    97	                        navigate('/courier/dashboard');
    98	                    } else if (userProfile.role === 'mediabuyer') {
    99	                        navigate('/mediabuyer/dashboard');
   100	                    } else {
   101	                        navigate('/');
   102	                    }
   103	                    return;
   104	                }
   105	            }
   106	
   107	            setRole(userProfile.role ?? null);
   108	            setPageMap((userProfile as { permissions?: { pages?: Record<string, boolean> | null } | null })
   109	                .permissions?.pages ?? undefined);
   110	            setAuthenticated(true);
   111	            setLoading(false);
   112	        };
   113	
   114	        checkAuth();
   115	
   116	        const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((event, session) => {
   117	            if (event === 'SIGNED_OUT') {
   118	                navigate('/auth');
   119	            } else if (session) {
   120	                // We might want to re-check role here if session changes, but for now basic auth check
   121	                setAuthenticated(true);
   122	            }
   123	        });
   124	
   125	        return () => subscription.unsubscribe();
   126	    }, [navigate, allowedRoles]);
   127	
   128	    if (loading) {
   129	        return <FullscreenLoader sublabel="Nous vérifions vos autorisations." />;
   130	    }
   131	
   132	    if (!authenticated) return null;
   133	
   134	    // Page gate — role 'admin' ONLY. Every other role passes through untouched.
   135	    if (role === 'admin' && !isPageAllowed(location.pathname, pageMap)) {
   136	        const landing = firstAllowedPath(pageMap);
   137	        if (!landing) return <NoAllowedPages />;
   138	        return <Navigate to={landing} replace />;
   139	    }
   140	
   141	    return (
   142	        <>
   143	            {children}
   144	            <AnnouncementGate />
   145	        </>
   146	    );
   147	};
   100	// Page 3: seller (agent) inbox — WA-verbatim tier, assignment-scoped via RLS.
   101	const WhatsappSellerInbox = lazy(() => import("@/whatsapp/WhatsappSellerInbox"));

   115	const BusinessGuard = ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => {
   116	  const [ready, setReady] = useState(() => {
   117	    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
   118	    const qsId = params.get('businessId');
   119	    if (qsId) {
   120	      localStorage.setItem('activeBusinessId', qsId);
   121	      window.history.replaceState({}, '', window.location.pathname);
   122	      return true;
   123	    }
   124	    return !!localStorage.getItem('activeBusinessId');
   125	  });
   126	  const [businessStatus, setBusinessStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);
   127	  const [userRole, setUserRole] = useState<string | null>(null);
   128	  const [businessId, setBusinessId] = useState<string | null>(null);
   129	
   130	  useEffect(() => {
   131	    const resolve = async () => {
   132	      // getSession (local JWT) not getUser (network→GoTrue): ProtectedRoute already
   133	      // validated the session upstream, and RLS enforces on the selects below.
   134	      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
   135	      const user = session?.user;
   136	      if (!user) return;
   137	
   138	      const { data: profile } = await supabase
   139	        .from('users')
   140	        .select('"DigiCom Business", role')
   141	        .eq('id', user.id)
   142	        .single();
   143	
   144	      if (profile?.role) setUserRole(profile.role);
   145	
   146	      if (!ready && profile?.['DigiCom Business']) {
   147	        localStorage.setItem('activeBusinessId', profile['DigiCom Business']);
   148	      }
   149	
   150	      const bid = localStorage.getItem('activeBusinessId');
   151	      if (bid) {
   152	        setBusinessId(bid);
   153	        const { data: business } = await supabase
   154	          .from('businesses')
   155	          .select('status')
   156	          .eq('id', bid)
   157	          .single();
   158	
   159	        setBusinessStatus(business?.status || 'active');
   160	      }
   161	
   162	      setReady(true);
   163	    };
   164	
   165	    resolve();
   166	  }, [ready]);
   167	
   168	  if (!ready) {
   169	    return <FullscreenLoader sublabel="Nous vérifions votre session et chargeons votre espace." />;
   170	  }
   171	
   172	  const isSuspended = businessStatus && businessStatus !== 'active' && userRole !== 'owner';
   173	
   174	  return (
   175	    <>
   176	      {children}
   177	      {isSuspended && businessId && (
   178	        <SuspensionOverlay role="admin" businessId={businessId} />
   179	      )}
   180	    </>
     1	// IGF IDENTITY ADAPTER — replaces WA Mancer's lib/auth.tsx (sanctioned seam #3).
     2	//
     3	// WA Mancer shipped a full AuthProvider (profiles table + localStorage cache).
     4	// iGrowFast owns identity (public.users, 6-role model, ProtectedRoute guards),
     5	// so this module keeps ONLY the consumer-facing contract the inbox files were
     6	// built against — same import path ('../lib/auth'), same useAuth() shape:
     7	//
     8	//   { session, profile, loading, profileLoading, revalidating,
     9	//     signIn, signOut, refreshProfile }
    10	//
    11	// profile is the WA `Profile` row shape (whatsapp.profiles view contract),
    12	// mapped from public.users:
    13	//   full_name    <- users.name
    14	//   role         <- {'super admin','business owner','admin'} -> 'admin', else 'user'
    15	//                   (WA user_role value space; every UI gate compares === 'admin',
    16	//                    parity with public.current_user_is_business_admin())
    17	//   is_active    <- users.active
    18	//   avatar_config passthrough (public.users.avatar_config jsonb)
    19	//   avatar_url   passthrough (public.users.avatar_url — uploaded photo; IGF
    20	//                 precedence photo → config → fallback, see UserAvatar.tsx)
    21	//
    22	// No provider needed: session comes from the single IGF GoTrue client, the
    23	// profile row through TanStack Query (WaShell's waQueryClient). Consumers all
    24	// null-guard (`session?.user.id`, `profile?.role`), so the brief async gap on
    25	// first mount is safe.
    26	import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
    27	import type { Session } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
    28	import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
    29	import { supabase } from './supabase'
    30	import type { Profile } from './database.types'
    31	
    32	const PROFILE_KEY = ['wa-current-profile'] as const
    33	
    34	// Module-level session mirror so remounts render with the last-known session
    35	// immediately instead of a null flash on every navigation.
    36	let cachedSession: Session | null = null
    37	
    38	function mapUsersRow(row: {
    39	  id: string
    40	  name: string | null
    41	  email: string | null
    42	  role: string | null
    43	  active: boolean | null
    44	  avatar_config: unknown
    45	  avatar_url: string | null
    46	  created_at: string | null
    47	}): Profile {
    48	  return {
    49	    id: row.id,
    50	    email: row.email ?? '',
    51	    full_name: row.name,
    52	    role: row.role === 'super admin' || row.role === 'business owner' || row.role === 'admin' ? 'admin' : 'user',
    53	    is_active: row.active ?? true,
    54	    avatar_config: (row.avatar_config ?? null) as Profile['avatar_config'],
    55	    avatar_url: row.avatar_url ?? null,
    56	    created_at: row.created_at ?? '',
    57	    last_auto_assigned_at: null,
    58	    updated_at: row.created_at ?? '',
    59	  }
    60	}
    61	
    62	async function fetchProfile(userId: string): Promise<Profile | null> {
    63	  const { data, error } = await supabase
    64	    .from('users')
    65	    .select('id, name, email, role, active, avatar_config, avatar_url, created_at')
    66	    .eq('id', userId)
    67	    .maybeSingle()
    68	  if (error) {
    69	    console.warn('wa profile fetch error', error)
    70	    return null
    71	  }
    72	  return data ? mapUsersRow(data) : null
    73	}
    74	
    75	export function useAuth() {
    76	  const qc = useQueryClient()
    77	  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(cachedSession)
    78	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(cachedSession === null)
    79	
    80	  useEffect(() => {
    81	    let cancelled = false
    82	    void supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data }) => {
    83	      if (cancelled) return
    84	      cachedSession = data.session
    85	      setSession(data.session)
    86	      setLoading(false)
    87	    })
    88	    const { data: sub } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, next) => {
    89	      if (cancelled) return
    90	      cachedSession = next
    91	      setSession(next)
    92	      setLoading(false)
    93	    })
    94	    return () => {
    95	      cancelled = true
    96	      sub.subscription.unsubscribe()
    97	    }
    98	  }, [])
    99	
   100	  const uid = session?.user.id ?? null
   101	  const profileQuery = useQuery({
   102	    queryKey: [...PROFILE_KEY, uid],
   103	    enabled: uid !== null,
   104	    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
   105	    queryFn: () => fetchProfile(uid as string),
   106	    meta: { silent: true },
   107	  })
   108	
   109	  const signIn = useCallback(async (email: string, password: string) => {
   110	    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password })
   111	    return { error: error?.message ?? null }
   112	  }, [])
   113	
   114	  const signOut = useCallback(async () => {
   115	    await supabase.auth.signOut()
   116	  }, [])
   117	
   118	  const refreshProfile = useCallback(async () => {
   119	    await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: PROFILE_KEY })
   120	  }, [qc])
   121	
   122	  return {
   123	    session,
   124	    profile: (profileQuery.data ?? null) as Profile | null,
   125	    loading,
   126	    profileLoading: profileQuery.isLoading,
   127	    revalidating: profileQuery.isFetching && !profileQuery.isLoading,
   128	    signIn,
   129	    signOut,
   130	    refreshProfile,

  2829	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  2830	-- 4. Structural rule: at most one business owner per business.
  2831	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  2832	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS users_one_business_owner_per_biz
  2833	  ON public.users ("DigiCom Business")
  2834	  WHERE role = 'business owner';
  2835	
  2836	ALTER TABLE public.users ADD CONSTRAINT users_business_owner_needs_business CHECK (role <> 'business owner' OR "DigiCom Business" IS NOT NULL);
  2837	
  2838	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  2839	-- 5. Protection trigger: the business owner cannot be demoted (role changed
  2840	--    away from 'business owner') or deleted by a peer. SECURITY DEFINER so
  2841	--    it can read the acting user's own role row regardless of RLS; search_path
  2842	--    pinned; EXECUTE revoked from PUBLIC/anon/authenticated (callers reach it
  2843	--    only via the trigger, never directly) — box default ACL grants EXECUTE
  2844	--    to everyone by default, so this revoke is not optional.
  2845	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  2846	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.protect_business_owner_role()
  2847	RETURNS trigger
  2848	LANGUAGE plpgsql
  2849	SECURITY DEFINER
  2850	SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_catalog'
  2851	AS $$
  2852	DECLARE
  2853	    v_actor_role text;
  2854	    v_demote_or_delete boolean;
  2855	    v_become_owner boolean;
  2856	BEGIN
  2857	    IF TG_RELID <> 'public.users'::regclass THEN
  2858	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'protect_business_owner_role: unexpected relation %', TG_RELID;
  2859	    END IF;
  2860	
  2861	    v_demote_or_delete :=
  2862	        (TG_OP = 'DELETE' AND OLD.role = 'business owner')
  2863	        OR (TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND OLD.role = 'business owner' AND NEW.role IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.role);
  2864	    v_become_owner :=
  2865	        TG_OP IN ('INSERT', 'UPDATE') AND NEW.role = 'business owner'
  2866	        AND (TG_OP = 'INSERT' OR OLD.role IS DISTINCT FROM 'business owner');
  2867	
  2868	    -- Fast path: the vast majority of users writes touch neither guard.
  2869	    IF NOT (v_demote_or_delete OR v_become_owner) THEN
  2870	        RETURN CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD ELSE NEW END;
  2871	    END IF;
  2872	
  2873	    -- System/service-role sessions pass (edge fns enforce their own caller
  2874	    -- checks; migrations/backfills run as postgres).
  2875	    IF auth.uid() IS NULL THEN
  2876	        RETURN CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD ELSE NEW END;
  2877	    END IF;
  2878	
  2879	    SELECT role::text INTO v_actor_role FROM public.users WHERE id = auth.uid();
  2880	
  2881	    IF v_actor_role IS DISTINCT FROM 'owner' THEN
  2882	        IF v_demote_or_delete THEN
  2883	            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Le propriétaire de l''entreprise ne peut pas être modifié par un pair.';
  2884	        ELSE
  2885	            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Seule la plateforme peut désigner un propriétaire d''entreprise.';
  2886	        END IF;
  2887	    END IF;
  2888	
  2889	    RETURN CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD ELSE NEW END;
  2890	END;
  2891	$$;
  2892	
  2893	REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.protect_business_owner_role() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
  2894	
  2895	DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_protect_business_owner_role ON public.users;
  2896	DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_protect_business_owner ON public.users;
  2897	CREATE TRIGGER trg_protect_business_owner
  2898	    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON public.users
  2899	    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.protect_business_owner_role();
  2900	
  2901	-- ── whatsapp.profiles view (live-sweep miss, found during authoring) ────────
  2902	-- Two role arrays gate WhatsApp profile visibility/bucketing; without parity a
  2903	-- business owner disappears from the WhatsApp module entirely. Live def dumped
  2904	-- 2026-07-29; ONLY the two arrays changed. security_barrier preserved in WITH.
  2905	CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW whatsapp.profiles WITH (security_barrier = true) AS
    95	CREATE POLICY provider_webhooks_tenant_all ON public.provider_webhooks FOR ALL TO authenticated
    96	  USING ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id())
    97	  WITH CHECK ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id());
    98	
    99	DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Enable all for authenticated users" ON public.webhook_logs;
   100	CREATE POLICY webhook_logs_tenant_all ON public.webhook_logs FOR ALL TO authenticated
   101	  USING ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id())
   102	  WITH CHECK ("DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id());
   103	
   104	-- 4) users SELECT: same-tenant + self + owner (writes already role-gated) -----
   105	DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Profiles are viewable by everyone" ON public.users;
   106	CREATE POLICY users_tenant_select ON public.users FOR SELECT TO authenticated
   107	  USING (
   108	    id = auth.uid()
   109	    OR "DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id()
   110	    OR public.is_owner()
   111	  );
   112	
   113	-- 5) businesses SELECT: own business + owner (picker / owner consoles) ---------
   114	DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow authenticated read" ON public.businesses;
   115	CREATE POLICY businesses_tenant_select ON public.businesses FOR SELECT TO authenticated
   116	  USING (
   117	    id = public.current_user_digicom_business_id()
   118	    OR public.is_owner()
   119	  );

Memory `baileys-meta-exposure-model.md`: *\"Evolution API = Baileys = reverse-engineered WhatsApp Web, against WhatsApp ToS. The ban vector is traffic detectability.\"* No per-conversation Meta fee is paid. **Cost per number today = amortized VPS + storage + ban risk, nothing else.**\n- **THE PRICING TRAP:** a **Meta Cloud API cutover is planned** (`public-website-decisions.md:26`: *\"Demos may show only Meta Cloud API-supported WhatsApp features\"*; `baileys-meta-exposure-model.md`: the policy wording *\"survives the Cloud API cutover with zero edits\"*). Under Cloud API, Meta bills **per conversation**, not per number. A tier metered on *number count* has ~zero marginal cost under Baileys and an **unbounded** one under Cloud API. The metering unit the owner picked is the one that breaks at the migration.\n- **Real capacity/storage evidence** (`docs/WA_ENTERPRISE_SCALE_PLAN.md:14`): *\"**Numbers per node** | node degrades at hundreds | Uncapped reconcile HTTP fan-out every 45s; **unbounded Evolution `Message` table (274 MB / 2 numbers / 5 mo)**.\"* → ≈137 MB per number per 5 months ≈ **~27 MB/number/month, growing unbounded**.      1	import React from 'react';
     2	import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
     3	import { AlertTriangle, Archive, Loader2, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
     4	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
     5	import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
     6	import {
     7	    Dialog,
     8	    DialogContent,
     9	    DialogDescription,
    10	    DialogFooter,
    11	    DialogHeader,
    12	    DialogTitle,
    13	} from '@/components/ui/dialog';
    14	import { Skeleton } from '@/components/ui/skeleton';
    15	import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
    16	
    17	/**
    18	 * Adaptive suppression dialog for the Users console.
    19	 *
    20	 * The server decides the face: `user_deletion_profile` (mig 20260730100000)
    21	 * counts the target's MEANINGFUL operational/financial references and returns
    22	 * `hard_deletable`. Zero references → the destructive face (real delete).
    23	 * Otherwise → the ARCHIVE face with receipts: load-bearing users are never
    24	 * hard-deleted (the payment ledgers cascade, so a "successful" delete would
    25	 * silently destroy money history).
    26	 */
    27	type DeletionProfile = {
    28	    commandes: number;
    29	    paiements: number;
    30	    colis: number;
    31	    notes: number;
    32	    campagnes: number;
    33	    demandes: number;
    34	    historique: number;
    35	    total: number;
    36	    hard_deletable: boolean;
    37	};
    38	
    39	const RECEIPT_ROWS: Array<{ key: keyof DeletionProfile; label: string }> = [
    40	    { key: 'commandes', label: 'Commandes' },
    41	    { key: 'paiements', label: 'Paiements' },
    42	    { key: 'colis', label: 'Colis' },
    43	    { key: 'notes', label: 'Notes' },
    44	    { key: 'campagnes', label: 'Campagnes' },
    45	    { key: 'demandes', label: 'Demandes' },
    46	    { key: 'historique', label: 'Historique' },
    47	];
    48	
    49	const DIALOG_SHELL =
    50	    'max-w-md p-0 border border-ant-line bg-ant-paper rounded-[20px] shadow-[0_32px_90px_-28px_rgba(20,20,19,0.4)] overflow-hidden max-h-[92vh] flex flex-col';
    51	
    52	export const DeleteOrArchiveUserDialog: React.FC<{
    53	    /** Target row — `null` keeps the dialog closed and the profile query idle. */
    54	    user: any | null;
    55	    onClose: () => void;
    56	    onHardDelete: () => void;
    57	    hardDeletePending: boolean;
    58	    onArchive: () => void;
    59	    archivePending: boolean;
    60	}> = ({ user, onClose, onHardDelete, hardDeletePending, onArchive, archivePending }) => {
    61	    const busy = hardDeletePending || archivePending;
    62	
    63	    const { data: profile, isLoading, error } = useQuery<DeletionProfile>({
    64	        queryKey: ['user-deletion-profile', user?.id],
    65	        enabled: !!user?.id,
    66	        gcTime: 0,          // every re-open re-reads the live reference counts
    67	        retry: false,
    68	        queryFn: async () => {
    69	            // RPC newer than the generated types → untyped rpc cast (house escape hatch).
    70	            // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
    71	            const { data, error: rpcError } = await (supabase as any).rpc('user_deletion_profile', {
    72	                p_user_id: user.id,
    73	            });
    74	            if (rpcError) throw new Error(rpcError.message);
    75	            return data as DeletionProfile;
    76	        },
    77	    });
    78	
    79	    const receipts = profile
    80	        ? RECEIPT_ROWS.map((r) => ({ ...r, count: Number(profile[r.key] ?? 0) })).filter((r) => r.count > 0)
    81	        : [];
    82	
    83	    const face: 'loading' | 'error' | 'delete' | 'archive' =
    84	        isLoading || !user ? 'loading'
    85	            : error ? 'error'
    86	                : profile?.hard_deletable ? 'delete'
    87	                    : 'archive';
    88	
    89	    const hairline =
    90	        face === 'archive' ? 'from-ant-orange via-ant-orange/60'
    91	            : face === 'loading' ? 'from-ant-ink via-ant-ink/40'
    92	                : 'from-ant-rose via-ant-rose/60';
    93	
    94	    return (
    95	        <Dialog open={!!user} onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open && !busy) onClose(); }}>
    96	            <DialogContent className={DIALOG_SHELL}>
    97	                {/* Gradient hairline — contextual hue: ink analysing / orange archive / rose destructive */}
    98	                <div className={cn('h-[3px] w-full bg-gradient-to-r to-transparent', hairline)} />
    99	
   100	                <DialogHeader className="px-6 pt-5 pb-5 border-b border-ant-line space-y-4">
   101	                    <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
   102	                        <div
   103	                            className={cn(
   104	                                'w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm',
   105	                                face === 'archive' || face === 'loading' ? 'bg-ant-ink text-white' : 'bg-ant-rose text-white',
   106	                            )}
   107	                        >
   108	                            {face === 'loading' && <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" strokeWidth={1.75} />}
   109	                            {face === 'archive' && <Archive className="w-5 h-5" strokeWidth={1.75} />}
   110	                            {face === 'delete' && <Trash2 className="w-5 h-5" strokeWidth={1.75} />}
   111	                            {face === 'error' && <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5" strokeWidth={1.75} />}
   112	                        </div>
   113	                        <div className="min-w-0">
   114	                            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.28em] text-ant-mid">
   115	                                <span
   116	                                    className={cn(
   117	                                        'h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full',
   118	                                        face === 'archive' ? 'bg-ant-orange' : face === 'loading' ? 'bg-ant-ink' : 'bg-ant-rose',
   119	                                    )}
   120	                                />
   121	                                {face === 'loading' ? 'Vérification'
   122	                                    : face === 'archive' ? 'Archivage'
   123	                                        : face === 'delete' ? 'Action irréversible'
   124	                                            : 'Vérification impossible'}
   125	                            </div>
   126	                            <DialogTitle className="font-display text-[20px] leading-tight tracking-tight text-ant-ink mt-1">
   127	                                {face === 'loading' ? <>Analyse de l&rsquo;activité&nbsp;…</>
   128	                                    : face === 'archive' ? <>Cet utilisateur a un historique d&rsquo;activité</>
   129	                                        : face === 'delete' ? <>Supprimer cet utilisateur&nbsp;?</>
   130	                                            : <>Vérification impossible</>}
   131	                            </DialogTitle>
   132	                            <DialogDescription className="font-serif italic text-[12.5px] text-ant-mid mt-0.5">
   133	                                {face === 'loading' ? (
   134	                                    <>Nous comptons les références de <span className="not-italic font-semibold text-ant-ink">« {user?.name} »</span> avant de choisir l&rsquo;action.</>
   135	                                ) : face === 'archive' ? (
   136	                                    <><span className="not-italic font-semibold text-ant-ink">« {user?.name} »</span> est rattaché à des données opérationnelles&nbsp;: la suppression définitive est impossible.</>
   137	                                ) : face === 'delete' ? (
   138	                                    <><span className="not-italic font-semibold text-ant-ink">« {user?.name} »</span> n&rsquo;a aucune référence opérationnelle. Le compte sera retiré de l&rsquo;authentification et de la base.</>
   139	                                ) : (
   140	                                    <>L&rsquo;activité de <span className="not-italic font-semibold text-ant-ink">« {user?.name} »</span> n&rsquo;a pas pu être vérifiée. Aucune action n&rsquo;a été effectuée.</>
   141	                                )}
   142	                            </DialogDescription>
   143	                        </div>
   144	                    </div>
   145	                </DialogHeader>
   146	
   147	                {face === 'loading' && (
   148	                    <div className="px-6 py-5 space-y-2">
   149	                        <Skeleton className="h-9 rounded-xl" />
   150	                        <Skeleton className="h-9 rounded-xl" />
   151	                        <Skeleton className="h-9 w-2/3 rounded-xl" />
   152	                    </div>
   153	                )}
   154	
   155	                {face === 'error' && (
   156	                    <div className="px-6 py-5">
   157	                        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-ant-rose/25 bg-ant-rose/5 px-4 py-3.5">
   158	                            <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-ant-rose">Erreur</p>
   159	                            <p className="text-[12.5px] text-ant-ink leading-snug mt-1 break-words">

   161	                            </p>
   162	                        </div>
   163	                    </div>
   164	                )}
   165	
   166	                {face === 'archive' && (
   167	                    <div className="ant-scroll flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto px-6 py-5 space-y-4">
   168	                        <div className="space-y-2">

   480	            if (isBO) {

   990	    meta,

  1089	                            {canEdit && (
  1090	                                <>
   600	
   601	      // Soft delete (second arg true): kills sessions + blocks login, keeps the
   602	      // auth row and reserves the email. If it fails after the public archive
   603	      // already succeeded, no revert is needed — active=false already blocks
   604	      // the app via the existing suspension handling, so it's fail-safe.
   605	      const { error: authError } = await supabaseClient.auth.admin.deleteUser(userId, true);
   606	      if (authError) {
   607	        console.error("archive: soft auth delete failed after public archive succeeded", userId, authError);
   608	        return json({ success: true, auth_soft_delete: false });
   609	      }
   610	
   611	      return json({ success: true });
   612	    }
   613	
   614	    if (action === "set_password") {
   615	      const { user_id, new_password } = body;
   616	      if (typeof new_password !== "string" || new_password.length < 8) {
   617	        return json({ error: "Mot de passe trop court (8 caractères minimum)." }, 400);
   618	      }
   619	      const { data: targetUser } = await supabaseClient
   620	        .from("users").select('"DigiCom Business", archived_at').eq("id", user_id).maybeSingle();

   623	      }

   627	        if (!sameBiz) return json({ error: "forbidden" }, 403);

   631	      if (pwError) throw friendlyError(pwError, "password");

   637	      headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   638	      status: 400,
   639	    });

   641	    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: (error as Error).message }), {

   643	      status: 400,

     1	// @ts-nocheck
     2	// Admin-gated: activate / deactivate a user account, then email account_reactivated /
     3	// account_suspended (with the admin-supplied reason). The status flip is the primary action;
     4	// the email is best-effort (a mail failure won't undo the flip). Caller must be a super admin /
     5	// admin in the SAME business as the target user.
     6	import "jsr:@supabase/functions-js/edge-runtime.d.ts";
     7	import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2.39.3";
     8	
     9	const corsHeaders = {
    10	  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
    11	  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
    12	};
    13	const json = (o: unknown, s: number) =>
    14	  new Response(JSON.stringify(o), { headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" }, status: s });
    15	
    16	const ROLE_FR: Record<string, string> = {
    17	  "super admin": "Super administrateur", "business owner": "Propriétaire d'entreprise", "admin": "Administrateur", "commercial": "Commercial",
    18	  "courier": "Livreur", "mediabuyer": "Media buyer", "owner": "Propriétaire",
    19	};
    20	const BIZ_OWNER_PROTECTED_MSG = "Le propriétaire de l'entreprise ne peut être géré que par un propriétaire ou la plateforme.";
    21	
    22	Deno.serve(async (req) => {
    23	  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
    24	
    25	  try {
    26	    const url = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "";
    27	    const SERVICE = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "";
    28	    const service = createClient(url, SERVICE, { auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false } });
    29	
    30	    const jwt = (req.headers.get("Authorization") ?? "").replace(/^bearer\s+/i, "").trim();
    31	    const { data: { user }, error: userErr } = await service.auth.getUser(jwt);
    32	    if (userErr || !user) return json({ ok: false, reason: "not_authenticated" }, 401);
    33	
    34	    const { data: me } = await service.from("users").select('role, "DigiCom Business", active').eq("id", user.id).single();
    35	    if (!me) return json({ ok: false, reason: "profile_not_found" }, 403);
    36	    if (me.active === false) return json({ ok: false, reason: "account_disabled" }, 403);
    37	    if (!["super admin", "business owner", "admin"].includes(String(me.role))) return json({ ok: false, reason: "forbidden" }, 403);
    38	    const businessId = me["DigiCom Business"];
    39	    if (!businessId) return json({ ok: false, reason: "no_business" }, 400);
    40	
    41	    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    42	    const userId = String(body?.user_id ?? "");
    43	    if (!userId) return json({ ok: false, reason: "missing_user" }, 400);
    44	    const active = body?.active === true;
    45	    let reason = String(body?.reason ?? "").trim();
    46	    const origin = typeof body?.origin === "string" && /^https?:\/\//.test(body.origin)
    47	      ? body.origin.replace(/\/$/, "") : (Deno.env.get("SITE_URL") || "https://igrowfast.com").replace(/\/$/, "");
    48	
    49	    // Target must belong to the caller's business.
    50	    const { data: target } = await service.from("users")
    51	      .select('id, name, email, role, "DigiCom Business", archived_at').eq("id", userId).maybeSingle();
    52	    if (!target) return json({ ok: false, reason: "not_found" }, 404);
    53	    if (target["DigiCom Business"] !== businessId) return json({ ok: false, reason: "forbidden" }, 403);
    54	    if (userId === user.id) return json({ ok: false, reason: "cannot_change_self" }, 400);
    55	    if (target.archived_at) {
    56	      return json({ ok: false, reason: "archived", error: "Utilisateur archivé — aucune modification possible." }, 403);
    57	    }
    58	    // Target already verified same-business above. No last-BO check —
    59	    // suspension is reversible.
    60	    if (target.role === "business owner" && me.role !== "owner" && me.role !== "business owner") {
    61	      return json({ ok: false, reason: "forbidden", error: BIZ_OWNER_PROTECTED_MSG }, 403);
    62	    }
    63	
    64	    // Primary action: flip the status (mirrors the previous direct update, now server-side).
    65	    const { error: updErr } = await service.from("users").update({ active }).eq("id", userId);
    66	    if (updErr) return json({ ok: false, reason: "update_failed", detail: updErr.message }, 400);
    67	
    68	    // Best-effort notification.
    69	    let emailWarning: string | null = null;
    70	    if (target.email) {
    71	      const { data: biz } = await service.from("businesses").select("name").eq("id", businessId).maybeSingle();
    72	      const nowFr = (() => {
    73	        try { return new Date().toLocaleString("fr-FR", { timeZone: "Africa/Casablanca", dateStyle: "long", timeStyle: "short" }); }
    74	        catch { return new Date().toISOString(); }
    75	      })();
    76	      if (!reason) reason = active ? "Votre accès a été rétabli par l'administrateur." : "Non précisé.";
    77	      const key = active ? "account_reactivated" : "account_suspended";
    78	      const vars: Record<string, unknown> = {
    79	        user_name: target.name || "",
    80	        role_label: ROLE_FR[target.role] || String(target.role),
    81	        business_name: biz?.name || "votre entreprise",
    82	        reason,
    83	      };
    84	      if (active) { vars.reactivated_at = nowFr; vars.login_url = `${origin}/auth`; }
    85	      else { vars.suspended_at = nowFr; }
    86	
    87	      try {
    88	        const resp = await fetch(`${url}/functions/v1/send-email`, {
    89	          method: "POST",
    90	          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": `Bearer ${SERVICE}`, "apikey": SERVICE },
    91	          body: JSON.stringify({ key, to: target.email, user_id: userId, vars }),
    92	        });
    93	        const out = await resp.json().catch(() => ({}));
    94	        if (!resp.ok || !out?.ok) emailWarning = out?.detail || out?.error || `status ${resp.status}`;
    95	      } catch (e) {
    96	        emailWarning = (e as Error).message;
    97	      }
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    return json({ ok: true, active, email_warning: emailWarning }, 200);
   101	  } catch (e) {
   102	    return json({ ok: false, reason: "error", detail: (e as Error).message }, 500);
   103	  }
   104	});

    19	async function sendEmail(payload: Record<string, unknown>) {
    20	  const SERVICE = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "";
    21	  return fetch(`${Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")}/functions/v1/send-email`, {

    31	    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    32	    const token = String(body?.token ?? "");
    33	    if (!TOKEN_RE.test(token)) return json({ ok: false, reason: "invalid" }, 400);
    34	
    35	    const service = createClient(Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "", Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "", {
    36	      auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false },
    37	    });

    39	    const { data: row } = await service.from("credential_change_requests")

    45	    const { error: upErr } = await service.from("credential_change_requests")
    46	      .update({ otp_hash: `\\x${otpHash}`, otp_expires_at: new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60000).toISOString(), otp_attempts: 0 })
    47	      .eq("id", row.id);
    48	    if (upErr) return json({ ok: false, reason: "error", detail: upErr.message }, 500);
    49	
    50	    const { data: u } = await service.from("users").select("email, name").eq("id", row.user_id).single();
    51	    if (!u?.email) return json({ ok: false, reason: "no_email" }, 400);
    52	
    53	    await sendEmail({
    54	      key: "otp_code", to: u.email, user_id: row.user_id,
    55	      vars: { otp_code: otp, otp_ttl: "10 minutes", purpose: "Modification de vos identifiants", user_name: u.name || "", request_ip: clientIp(req) },
    56	    });
    57	    return json({ ok: true }, 200);
    58	  } catch (e) {
    59	    return json({ ok: false, reason: "error", detail: (e as Error).message }, 500);
    60	  }
    61	});
     1	// @ts-nocheck
     2	// Authenticated: sends a 6-digit OTP to the logged-in user's CURRENT email.
     3	// Used for in-profile email/password change AND email-2FA at login. No link.
     4	import "jsr:@supabase/functions-js/edge-runtime.d.ts";
     5	import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2.39.3";
     6	
     7	const corsHeaders = {
     8	  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
     9	  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
    10	};
    11	const json = (o: unknown, s: number) =>
    12	  new Response(JSON.stringify(o), { headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" }, status: s });
    13	const sha256Hex = async (s: string) =>
    14	  Array.from(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(s))))
    15	    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
    16	const randHex = () => {
    17	  const b = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
    18	  return Array.from(b).map((x) => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
    19	};
    20	const clientIp = (req: Request) => (req.headers.get("x-forwarded-for") || "").split(",")[0].trim();
    21	
    22	async function sendEmail(payload: Record<string, unknown>) {
    23	  const SERVICE = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "";
    24	  await fetch(`${Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")}/functions/v1/send-email`, {
    25	    method: "POST",
    26	    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": `Bearer ${SERVICE}`, "apikey": SERVICE },
    27	    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    28	  }).catch((e) => console.error("send-email call failed:", e));
    29	}
    30	
    31	Deno.serve(async (req) => {
    32	  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
    33	  try {
    34	    const service = createClient(Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "", Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "", {
    35	      auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false },
    36	    });
    37	    const jwt = (req.headers.get("Authorization") ?? "").replace(/^bearer\s+/i, "").trim();
    38	    const { data: { user }, error: uErr } = await service.auth.getUser(jwt);
    39	    if (uErr || !user) return json({ ok: false, reason: "unauthenticated" }, 401);
    40	
    41	    const { data: me } = await service.from("users").select("email, name").eq("id", user.id).single();
    42	    const email = me?.email || user.email;
    43	    if (!email) return json({ ok: false, reason: "no_email" }, 400);
    44	
    45	    const otp = String(Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000));
    46	    const otpHash = await sha256Hex(otp);
    47	    const { error: insErr } = await service.from("credential_change_requests").insert({
    48	      user_id: user.id, requested_by: user.id, token_hash: `\\x${randHex()}`,
    49	      otp_hash: `\\x${otpHash}`, otp_expires_at: new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60000).toISOString(), otp_attempts: 0,
    50	    });
    51	    if (insErr) return json({ ok: false, reason: "error", detail: insErr.message }, 500);
    52	
    53	    await sendEmail({
    54	      key: "otp_code", to: email, user_id: user.id,
    55	      vars: { otp_code: otp, otp_ttl: "10 minutes", purpose: "Sécurité du compte", user_name: me?.name || "", request_ip: clientIp(req) },
    56	    });
    57	    return json({ ok: true }, 200);
    58	  } catch (e) {
    59	    return json({ ok: false, reason: "error", detail: (e as Error).message }, 500);
    60	  }
    61	});
