     1	-- #45 (owner-decided 2026-07-17): dedicated `mediabuyer` Postgres role.
     2	-- Media buyers stop sharing the `authenticated` DB role: a GoTrue custom
     3	-- access token hook stamps role='mediabuyer' into their JWTs, and PostgREST/
     4	-- storage switch to this role. DENY-BY-DEFAULT: the role is NOT a member of
     5	-- `authenticated`; every grant/policy below is the complete MB surface
     6	-- (enumerated from the task #35/#28 request maps).
     7	-- Owner ruling: orders visibility = ATTRIBUTED ONLY (own entry-day×source
     8	-- pairs + own ad periods). Closes: business-revenue console reads, peer spend,
     9	-- seller earnings, products.cost_price, order_items.unit_price.
    10	-- Config flip (GoTrue env) is a separate deploy step — this migration is inert
    11	-- for live users until the hook is enabled.
    12	
    13	-- ── 1. Role ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    14	do $$ begin
    15	  if not exists (select 1 from pg_roles where rolname = 'mediabuyer') then
    16	    create role mediabuyer nologin;
    17	  end if;
    18	end $$;
    19	alter role mediabuyer set statement_timeout = '8s';
    20	alter role mediabuyer set idle_in_transaction_session_timeout = '60s';
    21	grant mediabuyer to authenticator;
    22	grant usage on schema public to mediabuyer;
    23	grant usage on schema storage to mediabuyer;
    24	grant usage on schema auth to mediabuyer;
    25	grant usage on schema extensions to mediabuyer;

    27	-- ── 2. Helper functions ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    28	-- Platform label → orders.source_name (the exact CASE map used by
    29	-- mb_dashboard_stats / ads_history_metrics — keep the three in sync).
    30	create or replace function public.mb_platform_source(p_platform text)
    31	returns text language sql immutable set search_path to ''
    32	as $$
    33	  select case lower(coalesce(p_platform, ''))
    34	    when 'whatsapp ads'  then 'whatsapp'
    35	    when 'messenger ads' then 'messenger'
    36	    when 'instagram ads' then 'instagram'
    37	    when 'facebook ads'  then 'facebook'
    38	    when 'google ads'    then 'google'
    39	    when 'tiktok ads'    then 'tiktok'
    40	    when 'native ads'    then 'native'
    41	    when 'snapchat ads'  then 'snapchat'
    42	    when 'twitter ads'   then 'twitter'
    43	    when 'bing ads'      then 'bing'
    44	    when 'pinterest ads' then 'pinterest'
    45	    when 'reddit ads'    then 'reddit'
    46	    else ''
    47	  end;
    48	$$;
    49	
    50	-- Is this order attributed to the CALLING media buyer? SECURITY DEFINER so the
    51	-- entries/ads lookups bypass RLS (no nested-policy evaluation). SUPERSET of
    52	-- both live attribution semantics: the client keys on the UTC day slice, the
    53	-- RPCs bucket on the Casablanca day — both admitted, so neither surface loses
    54	-- rows. Day-granular either way: business-wide revenue stays unreadable.
    55	create or replace function public.mb_order_attributed(p_business uuid, p_source text, p_ts timestamptz)
    56	returns boolean language sql stable security definer set search_path to ''
    57	as $$
    58	  select
    59	    exists (
    60	      select 1 from public.campaign_daily_entries e
    61	      where e."DigiCom Business" = p_business
    62	        and e.created_by = (select auth.uid())
    63	        and public.mb_platform_source(e.platform) = lower(coalesce(p_source, ''))
    64	        and e.entry_date in ((p_ts at time zone 'UTC')::date,
    65	                             (p_ts at time zone 'Africa/Casablanca')::date)
    66	    )
    67	    or exists (
    68	      select 1 from public.ads_history ah
    69	      where ah."DigiCom Business" = p_business
    70	        and ah.created_by = (select auth.uid())
    71	        and public.mb_platform_source(ah.method) = lower(coalesce(p_source, ''))
    72	        and (
    73	          (p_ts at time zone 'UTC')::date
    74	            between ah.start_date and coalesce(ah.end_date, (p_ts at time zone 'UTC')::date)
    75	          or (p_ts at time zone 'Africa/Casablanca')::date
    76	            between ah.start_date and coalesce(ah.end_date, (p_ts at time zone 'Africa/Casablanca')::date)
    77	        )
    78	    );
    79	$$;
    80	
    81	-- Held-source labels for CampaignForm: (source, holder, holder name) for the
    82	-- caller's business. Replaces the direct campaign_source_locks+users embed —
    83	-- under the mediabuyer role peer users rows are unreadable, so the name comes
    84	-- through this gated DEFINER instead. Admins use it too (one code path).
    85	create or replace function public.mb_held_sources(p_business uuid)
    86	returns table(source text, mediabuyer_id uuid, owner_name text)
    87	language sql stable security definer set search_path to ''
    88	as $$
    89	  select l.source, l.mediabuyer_id, u.name
    90	  from public.campaign_source_locks l
    91	  left join public.users u on u.id = l.mediabuyer_id
    92	  where l."DigiCom Business" = p_business
    93	    and p_business = public.current_user_digicom_business_id();
    94	$$;
    95	
    96	-- GoTrue custom access token hook: stamp role='mediabuyer' for MB users,
    97	-- leave every other user's claims untouched.
    98	create or replace function public.custom_access_token_hook(event jsonb)
    99	returns jsonb language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path to ''
   100	as $$
   101	declare
   102	  v_role text;
   103	begin
   104	  select u.role into v_role
   105	  from public.users u
   106	  where u.id = (event->>'user_id')::uuid;
   107	  if v_role = 'mediabuyer' then
   108	    event := jsonb_set(event, '{claims,role}', to_jsonb('mediabuyer'::text));
   109	  end if;
   110	  return event;
   111	end;
   112	$$;
   113	
   114	revoke execute on function public.mb_platform_source(text) from public, anon;
   115	revoke execute on function public.mb_order_attributed(uuid, text, timestamptz) from public, anon;
   116	revoke execute on function public.mb_held_sources(uuid) from public, anon;
   117	revoke execute on function public.custom_access_token_hook(jsonb) from public, anon, authenticated;
   118	grant execute on function public.mb_platform_source(text) to mediabuyer, authenticated;
   119	grant execute on function public.mb_order_attributed(uuid, text, timestamptz) to mediabuyer;
   120	grant execute on function public.mb_held_sources(uuid) to mediabuyer, authenticated;

web search: 
web search: site:github.com/supabase/auth "SoftDelete" email hashed user ID admin delete ...
codex
The core review is complete. The RPC and TypeScript counter match exactly, but the feature still fails security and correctness criteria because tenant authorization, stale-token authorization, FK completeness, and transaction boundaries are not closed.
Inspected all scoped diffs, the dialog, relevant authorization helpers, last-business-owner trigger, credential paths, generated FK relationships, and pinned SDK semantics. The required live SSH catalog query was attempted but blocked by the enforced network sandbox; recorded schema evidence was used with that limitation stated.
  ✓ Read required review/Supabase instructions and project capability context
  ✓ Inspect uncommitted migration, function diffs, dialog, dependency versions, and all unarchive paths
  ✓ Query live box read-only for FK/grant/RLS reality and compare deletion categories
  ✓ Report ranked adversarial findings with scenarios and one-line fixes
codex
FAIL

1. **Critical — cross-tenant deletion/archive and platform-owner deletion.** [manage-users](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/manage-users/index.ts:465>) checks tenant ownership only when the target is a `business owner`; ordinary users can be deleted or archived by an admin from another business. Delete also lacks the archive path’s `owner` protection, while the DB trigger bypasses service-role writes. A tenant admin who knows a foreign UUID can delete an unreferenced foreign user, leak reference counts for a referenced user, or attempt to delete the platform owner. **Fix:** centralize target authorization before dispatch: platform owner, otherwise exact same business; explicitly forbid managing role `owner` unless the caller is `owner`.

2. **Critical — archive does not terminate authorization.** Caller authorization in [manage-users](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/manage-users/index.ts:264>) ignores `active` and `archived_at`, while auth soft-delete failure returns success at [lines 605–608](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/manage-users/index.ts:605>). An archived admin whose GoTrue call fails can continue invoking privileged actions with the same bearer. Even successful user deletion does not invalidate an already-issued JWT, and the existing RLS helpers do not test archive status. [Supabase documents this JWT behavior](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/auth/managing-user-data#deleting-users). **Fix:** make central role/tenant helpers and every privileged edge caller require `active=true AND archived_at IS NULL`; do not report success for failed GoTrue cleanup without a durable retry job.

3. **Critical — count/delete TOCTOU can destroy newly-created financial rows.** [countUserReferences](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/manage-users/index.ts:93>) and public deletion at [line 508](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/manage-users/index.ts:508>) are separate requests/transactions. A `seller_payments` or `mediabuyer_payments` row inserted after the count but before deletion is then removed by its CASCADE FK. **Fix:** perform parent-row locking, eligibility counts, and public deletion in one database transaction/RPC; `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` the user before counting.

4. **High — the seven categories do not cover the user FK reality.** The SQL and TypeScript sets match each other exactly, but both omit operational or audit references present in the repo schema catalog: `orders.courier_id`, `orders.deleted_by_id`, `courier_payment_notes.created_by`, `business_payments.submitted_by/reviewed_by`, `seller_presences.seller_id`, `courier_fees.courier_id`, `courier_zones.courier_id`, `campaign_source_locks.mediabuyer_id`, and `businesses.creator_id`; see [database.types.ts](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/src/lib/database.types.ts:298>) and [orders relationships](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/src/lib/database.types.ts:3136>). A courier with assigned orders but no parcel, or a payment-note creator who is not its courier, returns `hard_deletable=true`; the delete then either loses attribution through SET NULL/CASCADE or fails despite the dialog promising deletion. **Fix:** expand the explicit count set and add one eligibility test for every live FK, with a documented allowlist only for genuinely disposable plumbing.

5. **High — last-business-owner safety is raceable and inconsistent.** The archive count correctly filters active, non-archived peers at [lines 581–588](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/manage-users/index.ts:581>), but two concurrent archives can both observe the other owner and leave zero. Delete and demotion counts at [lines 391–396](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/manage-users/index.ts:391>) and [479–484](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/manage-users/index.ts:479>) still count inactive/archived owners. The existing serialized trigger does not recognize archive transitions and also counts every owner regardless of status at [20260729180000](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/migrations/20260729180000_business_owner_multiplicity.sql:89>). **Fix:** archive through a caller-context RPC that locks the business row and treats archive as owner removal, counting only active, non-archived peers.

6. **High — archived-row guards are TOCTOU, so `active=true` can win after archive.** `manage-users` reads `archived_at`, then later performs an unconditional update; [account-status-change](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/account-status-change/index.ts:50>) does the same before updating `active` at line 65. A stale update can read an unarchived row, wait behind archive, then reactivate the now-archived row. `userData` also does not strip `archived_at`. **Fix:** add a DB CHECK `archived_at IS NULL OR active=false` plus a trigger freezing already-archived rows; strip system columns and make updates conditional on `archived_at IS NULL`.

7. **Medium — public-first hard deletion has no repair channel.** If public deletion succeeds and auth deletion fails at [lines 515–518](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/manage-users/index.ts:515>), the UI loses the target and cannot retry, while the auth account continues reserving state and possibly authenticating. **Fix:** write an auth-cleanup outbox record in the same public-delete transaction and retry idempotently until GoTrue confirms deletion.

8. **Medium — archived accounts remain mutable through credential flows.** [request-credential-change](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/request-credential-change/index.ts:54>) can issue a link for an archived target, and [confirm-credential-change](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/functions/confirm-credential-change/index.ts:67>) later changes its auth credentials/profile email without checking archive state. Outstanding requests also survive archive. **Fix:** gate request, OTP, and confirmation on active/non-archived status and revoke outstanding credential requests during archive.

9. **Medium — migration and archive replay are not idempotent.** `ALTER TABLE … ADD COLUMN archived_at` at [migration line 14](</Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast/supabase/migrations/20260730100000_user_archive.sql:14>) fails on replay. Replaying `action=archive` overwrites the timestamp and repeats GoTrue deletion because it does not return early for an archived target. **Fix:** use `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS` and make archive return the existing archived result without mutating it.

Validated points:

- RPC and edge counts match category-for-category; there is no divergence between their 16 leaf queries.
- `auth.uid()` is not caller-supplied and cannot be changed through `p_user_id`; no spoofing hole was found.
- `PUBLIC` and `anon` execution are revoked correctly. The `service_role` grant is redundant because its `auth.uid()` is null, but it is not a bypass.
- `deleteUser(userId, true)` is supported by the pinned client API; the second argument is `shouldSoftDelete`. [Supabase API reference](https://supabase.com/docs/reference/javascript/auth-admin-deleteuser).
- The fixed search path prevents request-controlled resolution, but `public` precedes `pg_catalog` and most relations are unqualified. Prefer `search_path=''` with fully-qualified objects.
- UUID interpolation in `.or()` is raw PostgREST syntax. No successful deletion bypass was found because the same malformed value fails UUID comparisons, but every action should reject non-UUID input before building filters.

Live-box limitation: the required `~/.igrowfast/db.sh` FK query was attempted, but this sandbox denied SSH with `Operation not permitted`. Therefore current `ON DELETE` actions beyond the supplied facts and recorded schema evidence still require a live catalog recheck before shipping.
